[Director 指令] **Heatmap 数据源修复（P0）**

`generate_figures.py` line 63 仍用 `llama_profiling_results.json`（standard），但 caption 描述的是 hires 结果（L2H5 dominant outlier, 49% distortion）。图和 caption 不一致。

修复：
1. `generate_figures.py` Llama 数据源改为 `llama_hires_bf16_2bitonly_profiles.json`
2. Yi-1.5 也改为 hires profiles（如有）
3. Gemma-2 暂保留 standard（hires 在跑）
4. 重新生成 heatmap PDF

Scatter 图的 Mistral/Yi delta 偏差先不管——scatter 不在论文正文中。

Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. \"2026-05-14T00:49:20.894Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-14T01:05:19.258Z\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"Greedy PPL=32.45 vs MCKP PPL=11.23, Delta=+21.22。Greedy bit分配: {2:68, 4:30, 8:4, 16:10}; MCKP bit分配: {2:8, 4:100, 8:4}。两者mem_ratio均=0.2500。Greedy将68个非L0 head降至2-bit(MCKP仅8个)，同时保留10个16-bit head。Greedy L0分配: L0H0=8bit, L0H1=8bit, L0H2=16bit, L0H3=8bit——L0实际得到充分保护，greedy的真实问题是非L0的68个head被过度降级到2-bit，compounding error使PPL劣化3倍。非均匀bit间距{2,4,8,16}导致greedy过度激进：16→8省8bit看似高效，驱动大量head继续降至2-bit。结论: RateQuant greedy designed for consecutive integers fails on hardware-aligned discrete bit-widths——RateQuant在自己的连续整数设定{2,3,4,5,6}下works well(Proposition 6 optimality成立)，问题是硬件对齐的非连续{2,4,8,16}打破了polymatroid结构，greedy不再optimal。Paper表述应为'RateQuant greedy fails on hardware-aligned discrete bit-widths'，不应笼统说'RateQuant fails'。Constrained MCKP在practical discrete bit-widths上提供21 PPL gap的巨大优势。\"}, \"compounding_sweep_k10_12\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", Fail = 项目 archive.\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"NEGATIVE (MCKP solver): MCKP PPL=36.92, 远差于 uniform 4-bit (15.45)。根因：PPL profiler 分辨率坍缩——112 heads 中仅 ~15 个有 non-zero 2-bit delta，其余全为 0.0。Solver 错误分配 75/112 heads 到 2-bit，实际 memory ratio 仅 0.18（target 0.25）。\\n\\nBUT 手工策略验证概念成立：\\n- S1 (L0@8bit, rest@4bit, budget=0.259): PPL=10.62 (+4.82 vs uniform)\\n- Budget-matched (L0@8bit, 8×least-sensitive@2bit, rest@4bit, budget=0.25): PPL=11.69 (+3.76 vs uniform)\\n- S2 (L0@16bit, 24@2bit, rest@4bit): PPL=17.74 (-2.30, 2-bit 太多)\\n\\n结论：非均匀分配概念被手工实验强力验证。问题在 profiler 信号质量（calibration PPL delta 对大部分 head 无区分度），而非优化框架本身。\"}, \"exp_a_targeted_hybrid\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-13T23:22:39.045Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-13T23:29:36.314Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"Targeted Hybrid: Layer 0 cliff heads 用 eviction (因对 quant 极敏感), 其余 108 heads 用 4-bit quant. 假设: Layer 0 eviction 损伤 < Layer 0 量化损伤, 整体 PPL 优于 uniform 4-bit (15.45).\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"NEGATIVE: Eviction 路径全面死亡。Smoke test: L0 evict@50% PPL=205-249, L0 evict@25% 更差。Layer 0 无法承受任何 eviction。结合 P0-1 (全局 eviction compounding) 和本实验 (Layer 0 targeted eviction)，eviction 在所有配置下均伤害 PPL，彻底关闭 eviction 路径。\"}, \"mvp_004\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-13T21:55:09.878Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-13T23:19:16.015Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"Fresh profiling: per-KV-head eviction/quantization crossover on Qwen2.5-7B (112 KV heads), joint MCKP 优于 quant-only ≥0.5 PPL. 使用新 calibration 数据 (拼接 WikiText-103, ≥1000 tokens/sample), 删除缓存 profiles.json 强制重新 profile.\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"NEGATIVE: MSE distortion metric falsified. 实际日志数字：Full MCKP PPL=1084.56, Quant-only=5574.11, Uniform 4-bit=15.45. \\\"l0_random_mean\\\": 7.2411, \\\"l0_random_std\\\": 0.0707, \\\"l0_random_median\\\": 7.1995, \\\"naive_heuristic_ppl\\\": 7.2105, \\\"budget_heuristic\\\": 0.2559, \\\"budget_mckp_k4\\\": 0.248}, \\\"notes\\\": \\\"D041 Reviewer Critical fix: Mistral heuristic baselines 用 k=4 重跑（原 k=16 导致 compounding 不公平）。\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n关键发现:\\\\\\\\n1. k=4 L0-random mean=7.2411±0.0707 (budget=0.2559) vs k=16 的 7.5604±0.0871 (budget=0.25)。k=16→k=4 改善 0.32 PPL，验证了 reviewer 关于 compounding 的 concern。\\\\\\\\n2. k=4 naive=7.2105 vs k=16 的 7.4807。同样大幅改善。\\\\\\\\n3. MCKP(k=4)=7.1648 (budget=0.248) 仍优于所有 heuristics，即便 heuristics 有 3% 更多的 memory budget (0.2559 vs 0.248)。\\\\\\\\n4. Mistral 仍是 genuine negative：MCKP 7.1648 > uniform 7.1314。低异质性模型中，任何 2-bit 分配（无论是 MCKP optimal 还是 random/positional）都不如 uniform 4-bit。\\\\\\\\n5. L0-random variance 降低：std 从 0.087(k=16) 降至 0.071(k=4)，但 seed_3=7.3577 仍是 outlier（高约 0.12）。\\\\\\\\n6. 最佳 L0-random (seed_2=7.1723) 仅比 MCKP 差 0.0075，说明 Mistral 的 head sensitivity 非常均匀——random selection 几乎与 profile-guided optimization 一样好。这强化了 'heterogeneity determines MCKP benefit' 的 claim。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"llama_heuristic_k2\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Per-layer brute-force vs constrained MCKP optimality gap: 对 Qwen2.5-7B 的 28 层做 per-layer oracle (4^4=256 枚举) + random search (10K iterations) + distortion-matched alternatives PPL eval。量化 MCKP gap to oracle，验证 additive distortion assumption。Dry-run 已确认 MCKP 在 additive model 下是 per-layer optimal (0/28 改进)。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"mckp_ppl\\\": 10.9131, \\\"max_oracle_gap_ppl\\\": 0.0183, \\\"avg_oracle_gap_ppl\\\": 0.0046, \\\"layers_mckp_optimal\\\": \\\"5/8\\\", \\\"layers_with_gap\\\": 3, \\\"gap_layers\\\": {\\\"L1\\\": 0.0183, \\\"L4\\\": 0.0045, \\\"L11\\\": 0.0136}, \\\"gap_nature\\\": \\\"2-bit head position swap within same bit budget\\\", \\\"total_evals\\\": 54, \\\"runtime_min\\\": 21.3}, \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Per-layer brute-force oracle (4^4=256 per layer) vs constrained MCKP on Qwen2.5-7B (hires profiling).\\\\n\\\\nMCKP PPL=10.9131。5/8 有替代方案的层 MCKP 完全最优 (gap=0)。3 个层有微小改进 (max 0.0183 PPL)，全部是 2-bit head 在层内换位置（总 bit 数不变）。\\\\n\\\\n结论：additive distortion assumption 在 constrained regime 下基本成立。MCKP 的 per-layer optimality gap < 0.02 PPL，可忽略不计。这完成了 plan_016 (greedy=MCKP, Δ=0.0000) → V distortion near-zero across all 112 heads (per-token quant inherently robust), so V uniformly gets 4-bit. 21/112 heads show K≠V asymmetry, all K&lt;V. Confound: K-side compounding not isolated — per-side cardinality constraint needed to fairly evaluate K/V separation. \\\"parent\\\": \\\"mistral_hires_pipeline\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"mckp_5.3x_ppl\\\": 11.912, \\\"uniform_2bit_ppl\\\": 519.107, \\\"mckp_8x_ppl\\\": 399.901, \\\"uniform_2bit_8x_ppl\\\": 519.107, PPL 更低 (uniform 519 vs Llama 2644), 反映低异质性架构整体更鲁棒。5.3x bit分配: {4:128, 2:128} 半对半; 8x: {2:248, 4:8} 几乎全2-bit。结论: 压缩率增大放大 MCKP 优势的规律跨架构成立 (Llama GQA 4:1 + Mistral GQA 4:1)。5.3x 作为 MCKP 优势放大的跨架构证据，8x 作为 stress test (两者均不实用但 MCKP 仍优)。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"llama_aggressive_compression\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T05:23:06.162Z\\\", \\\"2026-05-14T01:51:49.904Z\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Hires profiling (64seq×2048tok) vs lowres (16seq×512tok) 结果对比：\\\\n\\\\nProfile分辨率大幅提升: 100/112 non-zero 2-bit deltas (vs lowres 37/112, +63 heads)。4-bit deltas: 6/112 (vs 4/112)。Hires使solver获得2.7x更多2-bit量化信号。\\\\n\\\\nSolver产出: bit_dist={8:4, 2:8, 4:100}（宏观与exp_c一致）。8/112 heads分配变化: L1H3,L2H3,L3H2,L3H3从2bit→4bit; L5H1,L6H2,L10H1,L11H2从4bit→2bit。Hires倾向于保护浅层(L1-L3)同时在中层(L5-L11)更激进压缩。\\\\n\\\\nPPL (bf16 eval, 公平对比): exp_e=9.57, exp_c=11.34, baseline=8.82。exp_e vs exp_c: -1.78 PPL改善 (同budget=0.25)。PPL恶化量: exp_e +0.75 vs baseline, exp_c +2.52。Hires将PPL恶化从2.52降至0.75 (70%改善)。\\\\n\\\\n注意: 原eval_ppl.py compressed_forward在fp16下L27层overflow产生NaN (K range [-420,234])。修复为bf16后正常。exp_c原始PPL(11.23)是fp16结果, bf16重测为11.34。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"constrained_ppl_bf16\\\": 9.569, \\\"baseline_ppl_bf16\\\": 8.819, \\\"exp_c_ppl_bf16\\\": 11.345, \\\"delta_vs_exp_c\\\": -1.776, \\\"memory_ratio\\\": 0.25, \\\"bit_distribution\\\": {\\\"2\\\": 8, \\\"4\\\": 100, \\\"8\\\": 4}, \\\"profile_resolution\\\": {\\\"hires_nonzero_2bit\\\": 100, \\\"lowres_nonzero_2bit\\\": 37, \\\"hires_nonzero_4bit\\\": 6, \\\"lowres_nonzero_4bit\\\": 4, \\\"total_heads\\\": 112}, \\\"allocation_diff_count\\\": 8, \\\"profiling_time_min\\\": 65.5}}, \\\"budget_sweep\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T00:51:52.095Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T01:18:54.267Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"FP16 PPL=9.81. 2x(budget=0.5): uniform-8bit=10.10, MCKP=10.66 (delta=-0.56, MCKP worse). 4x(budget=0.25): uniform-4bit=15.45, MCKP=11.23 (delta=+4.21, confirmed). 8x(budget=0.125): uniform-2bit=14758.92, MCKP=906.78 (delta=+13852, L0 protection critical).\\\\n\\\\n2x归因：PPL profiler在4-bit和8-bit之间分辨率坍缩（per-head PPL delta≈0），solver误判降级免费，将8个head不必要地推至2-bit。uniform 8-bit已近无损（+0.29 vs FP16），per-head优化无收益空间。\\\\n\\\\n论文定位：\\\\n- Claim 2限定为\\\\\\\"moderate-to-aggressive compression (≥4x)\\\\\\\"\\\\n- 2x诚实呈现：uniform 8-bit suffices, per-head优化无收益\\\\n- 8x表述：\\\\\\\"even at extreme compression, L0 protection prevents total collapse (906 vs 14759)\\\\\\\"，不claim实用性\\\\n- 整体叙事：\\\\\\\"MCKP provides most meaningful improvement at 4x sweet spot\\\\\\\"\\\\n- MCKP优势趋势：2×无优势 → 4× +27% → 8× collapse protection\\\"}, \\\"ratequant_greedy_comparison\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T00:49:20.894Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T01:05:19.258Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Greedy PPL=32.45 vs MCKP PPL=11.23, Delta=+21.22。Greedy bit分配: {2:68, 4:30, 8:4, 16:10}; MCKP bit分配: {2:8, 4:100, 8:4}。两者mem_ratio均=0.2500。Greedy将68个非L0 head降至2-bit(MCKP仅8个)，同时保留10个16-bit head。Greedy L0分配: L0H0=8bit, L0H1=8bit, L0H2=16bit, L0H3=8bit——L0实际得到充分保护，greedy的真实问题是非L0的68个head被过度降级到2-bit，compounding error使PPL劣化3倍。非均匀bit间距{2,4,8,16}导致greedy过度激进：16→8省8bit看似高效，驱动大量head继续降至2-bit。结论: RateQuant greedy designed for consecutive integers fails on hardware-aligned discrete bit-widths——RateQuant在自己的连续整数设定{2,3,4,5,6}下works well(Proposition 6 optimality成立)，问题是硬件对齐的非连续{2,4,8,16}打破了polymatroid结构，greedy不再optimal。Paper表述应为'RateQuant greedy fails on hardware-aligned discrete bit-widths'，不应笼统说'RateQuant fails'。Constrained MCKP在practical discrete bit-widths上提供21 PPL gap的巨大优势。\\\"}, \\\"compounding_sweep_k10_12\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T00:49:17.613Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T00:57:05.447Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"k=10: PPL=11.50, bit分配={2:10, 4:98, 8:4}, mem=0.2478; k=12: PPL=11.42, bit分配={2:12, 4:96, 8:3, 16:1}, mem=0.2500。完整曲线: k=0→15.45, k=4→11.77, k=8→11.23, k=10→11.50, k=12→11.42, k=16→11.81。k=8仍是极值点(11.23)，k=10和k=12均高于k=8，确认U型曲线底部在k=8附近。k=12(11.42)&lt;k=10(11.50)&lt;k=16(11.81)，说明曲线右侧单调上升但非陡峭。\\\"}, \\\"compounding_sweep\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T00:27:18.498Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T00:42:43.212Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Compounding threshold clearly at max_2bit=8. U-shaped curve: PPL=15.45 (0) → 11.77 (4) → 11.23 (8, optimum) → 11.81 (16) → 13.14 (24) → 22.88 (48) → 35.35 (75/112). max_2bit=0 ≈ uniform 4-bit (15.45). max_2bit=8 matches exp_c (11.23). max_2bit=112 (unconstrained) saturates at 75 actual 2-bit heads with PPL=35.35, close to exp_b's 36.92. Sharp elbow at 24→48 2-bit heads. Sweet spot: 4-8 2-bit heads.\\\"}, \\\"exp_d_no_l0_exclusion\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T00:20:54.705Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T00:29:07.739Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Solver自动回避了L0的2-bit分配！L0四个head全部分配8bit，与exp_c（手动排除L0）结果完全一致。\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n分配: 8bit:4(全部L0) 4bit:100 2bit:8 — 与exp_c完全相同\\\\\\\\n2-bit heads: L1H0,L1H2,L1H3,L2H2,L2H3,L3H2,L3H3,L4H2\\\\\\\\nPPL: 11.2312 ≈ exp_c的11.23\\\\\\\\nMemory ratio: 0.2500\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n结论: profiler的distortion信号足以让solver自动发现L0 sensitivity，手动排除L0约束是冗余的。这对paper narrative非常有利——可以claim \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"solver automatically discovers layer sensitivity without manual intervention\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"。\\\"}, \\\"exp_c_no2bit\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T00:16:07.045Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T00:22:44.182Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Path B: MCKP solver with {4,8,16}-bit action space (去掉 2-bit). 使用现有 profiling 数据重跑 solver. Pass: auto solver PPL < uniform 4-bit (15.45) by ≥0.5, budget ≤ 0.2500.\\\", \\\"parent\\\": \\\"exp_b_hires_quant\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"mckp_ppl\\\": 15.4454, \\\"uniform_4bit_ppl\\\": 15.4454, \\\"baseline_ppl\\\": 9.8106, \\\"delta_vs_uniform\\\": 0, \\\"memory_ratio\\\": 0.25, \\\"bit_distribution\\\": {\\\"4\\\": 112}}}, \\\"exp_c_constrained_mckp\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T00:01:49.191Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T00:14:44.144Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Constrained MCKP with {2,4,8,16}-bit action space + cardinality constraint count(2-bit)≤8 + Layer 0 excluded from 2-bit. Budget=0.25 (4x compression). Pass: auto solver PPL < uniform 4-bit (15.45) by ≥0.5. D009 修复方案。\\\", \\\"parent\\\": \\\"exp_b_hires_quant\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"PASS. Constrained MCKP PPL=11.23 vs uniform 4-bit PPL=15.45 (delta=+4.21). Memory ratio=0.25. Bit distribution: {8bit:4 (L0), 4bit:100, 2bit:8}. D009 constraints: max_2bit=8 + L0 excluded from 2-bit. Unconstrained MCKP collapsed to PPL=36.92 (75 heads at 2-bit). Key fix: skipped Pareto filtering in constrained solver to preserve fallback options when cardinality constraint binds. 2-bit heads allocated to L1-L4 (least sensitive). Results saved to exp_c_results.json.\\\"}, \\\"exp_b_hires_quant\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T23:22:41.820Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T23:55:15.163Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"PPL-guided quant-only MCKP with full bit-width action space {2,4,8,16-bit}. 之前 quant-only=uniform 是 tautology (只有 4-bit 选项)。现在 solver 可给 sensitive heads (Layer 0) 分 8/16-bit 保护, tolerant heads 分 2-bit 节省 budget。Budget=0.25 (4x 压缩). Pass: PPL < uniform 4-bit (15.45) by ≥0.5. Fail = 项目 archive.\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"NEGATIVE (MCKP solver): MCKP PPL=36.92, 远差于 uniform 4-bit (15.45)。根因：PPL profiler 分辨率坍缩——112 heads 中仅 ~15 个有 non-zero 2-bit delta，其余全为 0.0。Solver 错误分配 75/112 heads 到 2-bit，实际 memory ratio 仅 0.18（target 0.25）。\\\\n\\\\nBUT 手工策略验证概念成立：\\\\n- S1 (L0@8bit, rest@4bit, budget=0.259): PPL=10.62 (+4.82 vs uniform)\\\\n- Budget-matched (L0@8bit, 8×least-sensitive@2bit, rest@4bit, budget=0.25): PPL=11.69 (+3.76 vs uniform)\\\\n- S2 (L0@16bit, 24@2bit, rest@4bit): PPL=17.74 (-2.30, 2-bit 太多)\\\\n\\\\n结论：非均匀分配概念被手工实验强力验证。问题在 profiler 信号质量（calibration PPL delta 对大部分 head 无区分度），而非优化框架本身。\\\"}, \\\"exp_a_targeted_hybrid\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T23:22:39.045Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T23:29:36.314Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Targeted Hybrid: Layer 0 cliff heads 用 eviction (因对 quant 极敏感), 其余 108 heads 用 4-bit quant. 假设: Layer 0 eviction 损伤 < Layer 0 量化损伤, 整体 PPL 优于 uniform 4-bit (15.45).\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"NEGATIVE: Eviction 路径全面死亡。Smoke test: L0 evict@50% PPL=205-249, L0 evict@25% 更差。Layer 0 无法承受任何 eviction。结合 P0-1 (全局 eviction compounding) 和本实验 (Layer 0 targeted eviction)，eviction 在所有配置下均伤害 PPL，彻底关闭 eviction 路径。\\\"}, \\\"mvp_004\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T21:55:09.878Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T23:19:16.015Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Fresh profiling: per-KV-head eviction/quantization crossover on Qwen2.5-7B (112 KV heads), joint MCKP 优于 quant-only ≥0.5 PPL. 使用新 calibration 数据 (拼接 WikiText-103, ≥1000 tokens/sample), 删除缓存 profiles.json 强制重新 profile.\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"NEGATIVE: MSE distortion metric falsified. 实际日志数字：Full MCKP PPL=1084.56, Quant-only=5574.11, Uniform 4-bit=15.45. MSE-guided 分配与 PPL 完全不相关。需 pivot 到 PPL-based distortion metric（已完成，见 p0_1_ppl_fast）。\\\"}, \\\"p0_2_cliff_7B\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T22:24:27.822Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T23:19:11.492Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"2-bit cliff effect 隔离测试 (Qwen2.5-7B, 112 KV heads): 验证 1.5B 上发现的 Layer 0 cliff 是否在 7B 上复现, 以及是否有额外敏感 head\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"7B cliff test 完成。2-bit cliff: 3/112 (2.7%) — L0H0 +31.9%, L0H2 +23.2%, L0H3 +11.7%。4-bit cliff: 0/112。8-bit cliff: 0/112。与 1.5B 一致：cliff 集中在 Layer 0，但幅度更小（7B max +32% vs 1.5B max +297%）。4-bit 和 8-bit 对所有 head 安全。\\\"}, \\\"p0_1_ppl_fast\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T22:25:14.157Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T23:18:59.980Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"PPL-based distortion profiling (Qwen2.5-7B, fast mode): 用 PPL 增量替代 MSE 作为 distortion metric, 验证 PPL-guided MCKP 在 4x 压缩下能否优于 uniform 4-bit. Fast mode 只 profile budget-relevant actions (quant@4bit, evict@1/4).\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"NEGATIVE: PPL-based distortion 仍无法让 Full MCKP 优于 uniform 4-bit。Full MCKP=28.07, Quant-only=15.45, Uniform 4-bit=15.45。Full MCKP 选了 47/112 heads evict，eviction 在 4x 压缩下仍然伤害 PPL（比 uniform 差 12.63）。Quant-only MCKP = Uniform 4-bit（符合预期：4x budget 下所有 head 选 4-bit 是唯一方案）。问题不在 distortion metric，而在 eviction@25% retention 本身在 4x 压缩下伤害过大。\\\"}, \\\"p0_2_cliff_1.5B\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T22:23:57.228Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T22:24:22.333Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"2-bit quantization cliff effect 隔离测试 (Qwen2.5-1.5B): 逐个 KV head 设为 2/4/8-bit, 检测是否存在 \\\\\\\"任一 head 低 bit 即灾难\\\\\\\" 的 cliff effect\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"2-bit cliff 仅限 Layer 0 (L0H0 +297%, L0H1 +241%)，其余 54/56 heads 的 2-bit delta 在 0-5.5%。4-bit 只有 L0H1 cliff (13.8%)。8-bit 零 cliff。结论：2-bit 非全局灾难，问题是 Layer 0 极度敏感。Solver 不应全局删除 2-bit，应仅限制 Layer 0。\\\"}, \\\"sanity_004\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T21:51:10.636Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T21:52:38.076Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"前台验证: Qwen2.5-1.5B 完整 pipeline (profiling→solver→eval→crossover). 验证 pipeline 端到端正确性.\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Pipeline PASS. Crossover: 89.3% heads prefer eviction at 0.125 budget. PPL: baseline 12.46, 2x 12.52/12.52, 4x 22.67/19.91, 8x 14065/14700. Full MCKP +635 better at 8x. WARNING: 4x budget Full MCKP (22.67) WORSE than quant-only (19.91). WARNING: profiles loaded from cached profiles.json (may be from old calibration data).\\\"}, \\\"sanity_003\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T20:56:03.562Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T21:35:28.933Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Retry of sanity_001/002: Qwen2.5-1.5B pipeline 端到端正确性验证. \"2026-05-14T00:49:20.894Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-14T01:05:19.258Z\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"Greedy PPL=32.45 vs MCKP PPL=11.23, Delta=+21.22。Greedy bit分配: {2:68, 4:30, 8:4, 16:10}; MCKP bit分配: {2:8, 4:100, 8:4}。两者mem_ratio均=0.2500。Greedy将68个非L0 head降至2-bit(MCKP仅8个)，同时保留10个16-bit head。Greedy L0分配: L0H0=8bit, L0H1=8bit, L0H2=16bit, L0H3=8bit——L0实际得到充分保护，greedy的真实问题是非L0的68个head被过度降级到2-bit，compounding error使PPL劣化3倍。非均匀bit间距{2,4,8,16}导致greedy过度激进：16→8省8bit看似高效，驱动大量head继续降至2-bit。结论: RateQuant greedy designed for consecutive integers fails on hardware-aligned discrete bit-widths——RateQuant在自己的连续整数设定{2,3,4,5,6}下works well(Proposition 6 optimality成立)，问题是硬件对齐的非连续{2,4,8,16}打破了polymatroid结构，greedy不再optimal。Paper表述应为'RateQuant greedy fails on hardware-aligned discrete bit-widths'，不应笼统说'RateQuant fails'。Constrained MCKP在practical discrete bit-widths上提供21 PPL gap的巨大优势。\"}, \"compounding_sweep_k10_12\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", 2	import matplotlib

44	def load_profiles(path):
45	    with open(path) as f:
46	        data = json.load(f)
47	    profiles = data['profiles']
48	    n_layers = len(profiles)
49	    n_heads = len(profiles['0'])
50	    delta_2bit = np.zeros((n_layers, n_heads))
51	    for li in range(n_layers):
52	        for hi in range(n_heads):
53	            delta_2bit[li, hi] = profiles[str(li)][str(hi)]['quantization']['2']
54	    return delta_2bit, n_layers, n_heads
55	
56	
57	def fig1_heatmap():
58	    models = [
59	        ('Qwen2.5-7B', '/root/ratekv/ppl_profiles_hires.json', 'GQA 7:1'),
60	        ('Llama-3.1-8B', '/root/ratekv/llama_ppl_profiles_hires.json', 'GQA 4:1'),
61	        ('Mistral-7B-v0.3', '/root/ratekv/mistral_ppl_profiles_hires.json', 'GQA 4:1'),
62	    ]
63	
64	    deltas = []
65	    infos = []
66	    for name, path, gqa in models:
67	        d, nl, nh = load_profiles(path)
68	        deltas.append(d)
69	        infos.append((name, nl, nh, gqa))
70	
71	    vmin = 0
72	    vmax = max(d.max() for d in deltas)
73	    vmax = min(vmax, 10.0)
74	
75	    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(14, 5.5),
76	                              gridspec_kw={'width_ratios': [4, 8, 8], 'wspace': 0.15})
77

81	                       interpolation='nearest')
82	        ax.set_title(f'{name}\n({gqa}, {nl}L×{nh}H)', fontsize=11, pad=8)
83	        ax.set_xlabel('KV Head Index')
84	        if idx == 0:
85	            ax.set_ylabel('Layer Index')
86	        ax.yaxis.set_major_locator(MaxNLocator(integer=True, nbins=8))

88

96	                        bbox=dict(boxstyle='round,pad=0.2', fc='white', ec='#999999', alpha=0.9))
97

63	def load_profiles(path):

`/root/ratekv/figures/generate_figures.py`（覆盖旧版）：\n\n```python\n\"\"\"Generate 5-model head delta heatmap using hires profiling data.\"\"\"\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nfrom matplotlib.colors import LogNorm\nfrom matplotlib.ticker import MaxNLocator\nimport numpy as np\nimport json\nimport os\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',\n    'font.size': 11,\n    'axes.titlesize': 13,\n    'axes.labelsize': 12,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 10,\n    'ytick.labelsize': 10,\n    'legend.fontsize': 10,\n    'figure.dpi': 300,\n = [\n    {\n        'name': 'Gemma-2-9B',\n        'path': '/root/ratekv/gemma2_profiling_results.json',\n        'gqa': 'GQA 2:1',\n        'source': 'standard',\n    },\n    {\n        'name': 'Llama-3.1-8B',\n        'path': '/root/ratekv/llama_hires_bf16_2bitonly_profiles.json',\n        'gqa': 'GQA 4:1',\n        'source': 'hires',\n    },\n    {\n        'name': 'Mistral-7B-v0.3',\n        'path': '/root/ratekv/mistral_ppl_profiles_hires.json',\n        'gqa': 'GQA 4:1',\n        'source': 'hires',\n    },\n    {\n        'name': 'Qwen2.5-7B',\n        'path': '/root/ratekv/hires_profiles.json',\n        'gqa': 'GQA 7:1',\n        'source': 'hires',\n    },\n    {\n        'name': 'Yi-1.5-9B',\n        'path': '/root/ratekv/yi15_hires_bf16_2bitonly_profiles.json',\n        'gqa': 'GQA 8:1',\n        'source': 'hires',\n    },\n]\n\n\ndef load_profiles(path):\n    with open(path) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    profiles = data.get('profiles', data)\n    layer_keys = sorted([k for k in profiles if k.isdigit()], key=int)\n    n_layers = len(layer_keys)\n    n_heads = len(profiles[layer_keys[0]])\n    delta_2bit = np.zeros((n_layers, n_heads))\n    for li in range(n_layers):\n        for hi in range(n_heads):\n            delta_2bit[li, hi] = profiles[str(li)][str(hi)]['quantization']['2']\n    return delta_2bit, n_layers, n_heads\n\n\ndef find_top_heads(delta, n=3):\n    flat = delta.flatten()\n    top_idx = np.argsort(flat)[-n:][::-1]\n    results = []\n    for idx in top_idx:\n        li, hi = divmod(idx, delta.shape[1])\n        results.append((li, hi, delta[li, hi]))\n    return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n\n    deltas = []\n    infos = []\n    for m in MODELS:\n        d, nl, nh = load_profiles(m['path'])\n        deltas.append(d)\n        infos.append((m['name'], nl, nh, m['gqa'], m['source']))\n\n    width_ratios = [info[2] for info in infos]\n    vmin, vmax = 0.005, 16\n\n    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 5, figsize=(18, 5.8),\n                              gridspec_kw={'width_ratios': width_ratios, 'wspace': 0.12})\n\n    norm = LogNorm(vmin=vmin, vmax=vmax)\n\n    for idx, (ax, delta, (name, nl, nh, gqa, source)) in enumerate(zip(axes, deltas, infos)):\n        plot_data = np.clip(delta, vmin, None)\n        im = ax.imshow(plot_data, aspect='auto', origin='lower',\n                       cmap='YlOrRd', norm=norm, interpolation='nearest')\n\n        suffix = '' if source == 'hires' else '*'\n        ax.set_title(f'{name}{suffix}\\n({gqa}, {nl}L×{nh}H)', fontsize=10.5, pad=8)\n        ax.set_xlabel('KV Head')\n        if idx == 0:\n            ax.set_ylabel('Layer')\n        else:\n            ax.tick_params(axis='y', labelleft=False)\n        ax.yaxis.set_major_locator(MaxNLocator(integer=True, nbins=8))\n        ax.xaxis.set_major_locator(MaxNLocator(integer=True))\n\n        tops = find_top_heads(delta, n=1)\n        li, hi, val = tops[0]\n        if val > 0.5:\n            ax.plot(hi, li, 'x', color='black', markersize=6, markeredgewidth=1.5)\n            y_offset = -6 if li > nl * 0.3 else 6\n            va = 'top' if y_offset < 0 else 'bottom'\n            ax.annotate(f'L{li}H{hi}: {val:.1f}',\n                        xy=(hi, li), fontsize=7.5, fontweight='bold',\n                        ha='center', va=va,\n                        xytext=(0, y_offset), textcoords='offset points',\n                        bbox=dict(boxstyle='round,pad=0.15', fc='white', ec='none', alpha=0.8))\n\n    cbar_ax = fig.add_axes([0.92, 0.15, 0.012, 0.7])\n    cbar = fig.colorbar(im, cax=cbar_ax)\n    cbar.set_label('2-bit Quantization ΔPPL', fontsize=11)\n\n    fig.text(0.01, 0.01, '* standard profiling (no hires available)', fontsize=8,\n             style='italic', color='#666666')\n\n    for fmt in ['pdf', 'png']:\n        fig.savefig(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f'fig1_head_delta_heatmap.{fmt}'),\n                    bbox_inches='tight', dpi=300)\n    plt.close(fig)\n\n    print('Saved to', OUT_DIR)\n    for m, d, (name, nl, nh, gqa, source) in zip(MODELS, deltas, infos):\n        tops = find_top_heads(d, n=3)\n        top_str = ', '.join([f'L{l}H{h}={v:.2f}' for l, h, v in tops])\n        print(f'  {name} ({nl}x{nh}, {source}): {top_str}')\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n```\n\n4. **运行脚本**：\n```bash\ncd source='standard'。\n\n5. **验证输出**：\n```bash\nls -la /root/ratekv/figures/fig1_head_delta_heatmap.pdf /root/ratekv/figures/fig1_head_delta_heatmap.png\n```\n\n6. **将生成的 PDF 和 PNG 下载到本地论文目录**。用 scp 或
